Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any physiology module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 193. The projective resolution has length at most 814 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the physiology decomposition. By minimality, the element a^193 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 814-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
